The guard refuses shell write-commands targeting those paths; reads pass, and tool-driven regeneration (chock sync) passes because it writes through the tool, not shell editing. Best-effort and deliberately coarse:… ([open-coder-ai/chock-codex-plugins](https://github.com/open-coder-ai/chock-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/codex/protect-ci-workflows))
- **protect-commit-privacy**: Keep the development conversation out of git history. Agent-authored commits narrate by default -- who asked for what, which discussion decided it, what the plan was -- and on a public repo that narration is published forever. The guard refuses git commit commands whose message (inline -m/--message or the file behind -F/--file) contains process-leak markers; the rule tells the agent to describe the change, not the conversation, and to propose sensitive messages to the human before committing. Best-effort: markers are a narrow deny-list, and a message the human explicitly approves can say anyt… ([open-coder-ai/chock-copilot-plugins](https://github.com/open-coder-ai/chock-copilot-plugins/tree/HEAD/claude/protect-commit-privacy))

- **protect-mcp**: MCP security gateway that wraps any MCP server with per-tool policies, Ed25519-signed decision receipts, and human approval gates. Shadow mode (default) logs every tool call without blocking. Enforce mode applies per-tool allow/deny/rate-limit/approval policies. Every decision is cryptographically signed and independently verifiable offline. MIT licensed. IETF Internet-Draft published for the receipts protocol. ([tomjwxf/protect-mcp-plugin](https://github.com/tomjwxf/protect-mcp-plugin))

- **proxyman**: Proxyman's official skills — install and setup, app settings, certificate lifecycle and recovery, HTTPS capture across devices and runtimes, the debugging tools (breakpoints, map local/remote, scripting, reverse proxy), licensing, and proxyman-cli. Note that unlike most vendors here the CLI does NOT replace the MCP: it covers proxy config, rules, certificates and logs, while live traffic inspection stays an MCP surface, which is why mcp-pack keeps the Proxyman server. ([ProxymanApp/proxyman-SKILL.md](https://github.com/ProxymanApp/proxyman-SKILL.md))
- **proxyme**: Spawn a read-only, ephemeral, one-shot proxy briefed with your identity — extracted from your real Claude Code sessions — that answers a single question with your authority and then terminates; purely reactive, scoped to your working directory, while the main agent does all the work. ([Korck-lab/proxyme](https://github.com/Korck-lab/proxyme/tree/HEAD/proxyme))
- **prozessrecht**: Prozessrechtliche Skills für Mandate, Fristen, Mahnbescheid, Eilverfahren, Vollstreckung und Schriftsätze. ([Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht](https://github.com/Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht/tree/HEAD/prozessrecht))
- **prozessrecht**: Zivilprozessrecht. Klageschrift §§ 253 ff. ZPO, Streitgegenstand, Sachvortrag und Beweisantritt, Zuständigkeit, Rechtsmittel, Vollstreckung. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/tree/HEAD/prozessrecht))
- **prp**: prp commands to ship products at rapid speed ([rpiplewar/shipfaster](https://github.com/rpiplewar/shipfaster/tree/HEAD/prp))
- **prp-core**: Complete PRP workflow automation as agent skills - prd, plan, implement, review, commit, PR, an autonomous build loop, and specialized agents ([Wirasm/prp](https://github.com/Wirasm/prp/tree/HEAD/plugins/prp-core))
- **prplos-dev**: prplOS firmware development toolkit: ODL parameter scaffolding skill, PostToolUse ODL syntax validation, and Playwright browser MCP server for web-UI testing. ([septimomend/claude-plugin-prplos-dev](https://github.com/septimomend/claude-plugin-prplos-dev))
- **prr**: AI-powered PR review with parallel multi-agent review and arbiter synthesis ([cfactolerin/prr](https://github.com/cfactolerin/prr))
- **prr**: Strategic product roadmapping guidance based on Product Roadmaps Relaunched — shifting from feature-date lists to outcome-driven, theme-based roadmaps that communicate strategy and rally stakeholders. ([dchuk/prr-plugin](https://github.com/dchuk/prr-plugin))
- **pruefungsrecht-hochschule**: Hochschulprüfungsrecht: Prüfungsordnung, Bewertungsspielraum, Akteneinsicht, Krankheit, Nachteilsausgleich, Täuschung, KI, Drittversuch und Eilrechtsschutz. ([Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht](https://github.com/Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht/tree/HEAD/pruefungsrecht-hochschule))
- **pruna**: Use when installing the full Pruna generative media suite — all guides, tools, and workflows in one package. ([PrunaAI/pruna-skills](https://github.com/PrunaAI/pruna-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/suite/pruna))
- **pruna-api**: Use before any Pruna or Replicate HTTP call — credentials, upload/poll/download, parallel batches, and agent safety. ([PrunaAI/pruna-skills](https://github.com/PrunaAI/pruna-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/guides/pruna-api))
- **prune**: Interactive branch cleanup — catalog, verify, and delete stale branches with squash-merge awareness. ([MattKotsenas/agent-plugins](https://github.com/MattKotsenas/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/prune))
- **prune**: Slash commands /prune:distill (transcript-bucketing prune), /prune:handover (in-context brief), /prune:resume (pick the newest brief back up, no filename to type), /prune:setup (onboarding diagnostic), an unattended tmux /clear → /prune:resume cycle, plus a statusline script that surfaces a visible nudge when context crosses the prune threshold. No background process, no MCP server — install this one for the everyday flow. ([neelts/prune-watch](https://github.com/neelts/prune-watch/tree/HEAD/prune))
- **prune-branches**: Delete local git branches whose content already landed on the default branch, with squash-merge detection and a dry-run preview. ([tehw0lf/claude-skills](https://github.com/tehw0lf/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/prune-branches))
- **prune-comments**: Documentation-only pass that fights comment-slop: drops, trims, moves, and de-duplicates comments and docstrings, leaving only the ones that earn their place. Never changes code. ([tsvikas/skills](https://github.com/tsvikas/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/prune-comments))
- **prune-watch**: Optional companion to the prune plugin. Adds the original channel watcher: a background MCP server that tails the active transcript and pushes a one-way &lt;channel&gt; nudge when context crosses ~200k tokens. Also exposes check/snooze/unsnooze MCP tools that /prune:distill integrates with automatically when this plugin is installed. Requires --dangerously-load-development-channels at claude launch to deliver nudges. ([neelts/prune-watch](https://github.com/neelts/prune-watch/tree/HEAD/watcher))
- **prxhub**: Cache-first access to the prxhub.com bundle registry. Search, download, cite, and publish verified .prx research bundles before spending compute on new research. Free to read, no account required. ([parallect/claude-code](https://github.com/parallect/claude-code/tree/HEAD/plugins/prxhub))
- **prymer**: Prymer shared context bus — connects the hosted Prymer MCP server, teaches the load-at-start / checkpoint-at-end ritual, and adds skills for loading, checkpointing, onboarding, curation, handoffs, the Projects produce-loop, and independent review, plus prymer-dispatch and prymer-project worker subagents. ([builtbyberry/prymer-claude-plugin](https://github.com/builtbyberry/prymer-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/prymer))
- **przypominamy**: Polish SMS gateway via hosted MCP. API key from app.przypominamy.com/keys. ([pawelmamcarz/przypominamy-grok-plugin](https://github.com/pawelmamcarz/przypominamy-grok-plugin))

- **ps-skills**: The all-in-one Process Street toolkit — build, discover, and convert-to-workflow skills, via the public API with your own key. ([process-street/ps-skills](https://github.com/process-street/ps-skills))
- **psake**: psake PowerShell build automation — task-based build scripts for .NET, Node.js, Docker, and more ([psake/psake-llm-tools](https://github.com/psake/psake-llm-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/psake))
- **psci-skills**: Twelve-skill bundle covering the Psychological Science manuscript lifecycle, including its short-report word format and post-2024 open-science requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Psychological-Science-Skills))
- **psd-coding-system**: AI-assisted development system — 3 disciplined surfaces (/plan, /lfg, /evolve), verification-first run-to-100%-clean PR loop, specialized agents, memory-based learning, and Context7 framework docs ([psd401/psd-claude-plugins](https://github.com/psd401/psd-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psd-coding-system))
- **psd-productivity**: 38 productivity workflows + 1 agent — Google-style tech writing, ParentSquare district data CLI, Atrium publishing (AI Studio docs + HTML artifacts), beautiful HTML artifact generation (incl. self-contained data dashboards & charts), board policy formatting, DocuSign migration/export, PDF Builder, Documenso document signing, n8n workflow automation, document generation, research, TTS, enrollment automation, browser control, Google Workspace, and district operations ([psd401/psd-claude-plugins](https://github.com/psd401/psd-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psd-productivity))
- **pseolint**: Full-lifecycle programmatic SEO, design, build, audit, fix, and gate template pages at scale without earning a thin-content / SpamBrain penalty. 12 playbooks, each bound to the pseolint rules that gate it. ([ouranos-labs/pseolint](https://github.com/ouranos-labs/pseolint/tree/HEAD/skills/pseolint))
- **psep**: 7 principles for architecture, design, and code review. /psep strict for full principles + decision framework, /psep relaxed for 7-question binary checklist. Stateless, scoped to design/review/refactor/architecture only. ([HanazonoArchive/PSEP](https://github.com/HanazonoArchive/PSEP))
- **pseudo-code-prompting-plugin**: Simplified pseudo-code transformation engine with stable specification reference. 6-step pipeline, architecture-aware generation, comprehensive validation across 6 dimensions, workflow coordination to prevent cc10x hijacking, mandatory specification-reference.md entry point, four-layer context protection (reference + markers + merging + recovery) for stable specification-driven TDD workflows. 140+ test cases validating stable context preservation. ([EladAriel/pseudo-code-prompting-plugin](https://github.com/EladAriel/pseudo-code-prompting-plugin))
- **pseudocodify**: v1.0.0 — Converts a codebase into language-agnostic pseudocode, for porting to a new language or documenting unfamiliar code ([keithmackay/pseudocodify](https://github.com/keithmackay/pseudocodify))
- **pseudolife-memory**: Wires the Pseudolife-MCP daemon into Claude Code: session briefing + identity hooks, /dream and /memory-status commands. MCP transport is registered separately (installer shim or claude mcp add). ([Pseudogiant-xr/Pseudolife-MCP](https://github.com/Pseudogiant-xr/Pseudolife-MCP/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **psim-mcp**: Altair PSIM 전력전자 시뮬레이션: MCP 서버 + 회로 워크플로/최적화 스킬 ([zongseung/psim-mcp](https://github.com/zongseung/psim-mcp))
- **psincraian-myfy**: Official plugins for Python framework development with Myfy, enabling rapid web application building in Claude Code. ([psincraian/myfy](https://github.com/psincraian/myfy))
- **pskoett-ai-skills**: A self-improving agent skill pipeline that prevents common AI coding agent failures. Includes 6 skills (plan-interview, intent-framed-agent, context-surfing, simplify-and-harden, self-improvement, agent-teams) and 5 subagents (simplify-auditor, harden-auditor, spec-auditor, context-monitor, self-improvement-logger). The pipeline catches scope drift, context degradation, security gaps, and repeated mistakes — each skill targets a specific failure mode and hands off cleanly to the next. ([anthropics/claude-plugins-community](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **pskoett-ai-skills**: Two-loop skill pipeline for Claude Code. Inner loop (plan-interview, intent-framed-agent, context-surfing, verify-gate, self-healing, simplify-and-harden) catches failures in-session — including active runtime recovery via self-healing when a command, test, build, helper, env, or external service fails mid-task. Outer loop (learning-aggregator, harness-updater, eval-creator, pre-flight-check) captures recurring patterns, promotes them to CLAUDE.md as permanent rules, and turns failures into regression tests. The harness gets stronger with every cycle. ([pskoett/pskoett-ai-skills](https://github.com/pskoett/pskoett-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **pskoett-self-improving-agent**: An AI agent system that iteratively improves its own performance and responses through feedback loops and self-evaluation. ([pskoett/self-improving-agen](https://github.com/pskoett/self-improving-agen))
- **psl**: Product Spec Language — canonical vocabulary tools for your codebase. Auto-loads when PSL.md is present. Slash commands for init, lookup, validate, and ticket generation. MCP server with resolve, validate, search, canonicalize, and list tools. ([fliptables/psl](https://github.com/fliptables/psl/tree/HEAD/packages/plugin))
- **pslog-workflow**: task→spec/brief→plan→코드 + 단계별 사람 승인 워크플로 ([ardenspace/pslog](https://github.com/ardenspace/pslog/tree/HEAD/plugins/pslog-workflow))
- **psn**: Agent skills for a PlayStation play-history export: produce/refresh it, read your taste profile, triage your backlog, and rank your wishlist by what you'll actually finish. ([t3chnaztea/awesome-psn-skills](https://github.com/t3chnaztea/awesome-psn-skills))
- **psoares-annotate**: Point at things on screen instead of describing them. /annotate opens the project's app in a Chrome window with a picker; each note becomes a print-screen of the element plus its details, filed into the project as a round. /annotations reads the round, acts on every note, and cleans up. ([ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psoares-annotate))
- **psoares-content-extract**: Capture raw content (metadata, transcript, frames) from YouTube videos and Instagram reels, analyze it in the current Claude session, download YouTube videos (full or trimmed), and extract multiple clips from long-form content (podcasts, courses) via a conversational brainstorm. Skills: view-youtube-video, view-instagram-reel, extract-content, clip-youtube-video, clip-video-parts. ([ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psoares-content-extract))
- **psoares-dev**: Development workflow tooling. Skills: create-github-issue for filing grounded, well-scoped GitHub issues from the codebase, and journal-session for recording what happened in a Claude session as a narrative project journal. ([ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psoares-dev))
- **psoares-statusline**: Gruvbox Material Dark statusline with directory, git branch, model, context bar, cost estimate, agent, and vim mode. ([ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psoares-statusline))
- **psoares-writing**: Writing and prose tooling. Includes the human-prose skill for humanizing AI output across languages. ([ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ps-dev-team/psoares-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/psoares-writing))
- **psotobverse-utils**: Claude-only orthogonal workflow layer (skills, commands /comprehend /coverage /cross-examine, additive policy-routing hooks, governance hooks, meta-learner, subagents). /coverage is a recall safety net against missing a relevant policy. Runs in Claude Code and Cowork. ([psotob91/psotobverse-utils](https://github.com/psotob91/psotobverse-utils))
- **psp**: A Pain Signal Profile is a five-part buying brief. It replaces a static ICP. ([cmj-hub/claude-psp](https://github.com/cmj-hub/claude-psp))
- **psql**: psql — PostgreSQL's interactive terminal client and SQL script runner. Use when connecting to or inspecting a Postgres DB from a terminal, running SQL scripts or one-off queries in shells/CI/agents (`-c`/`-f`/`-qAtX`/`--csv`), using meta-commands (the `\d` family, `\copy`, `\watch`, `\gexec`, `\if` scripting, `\pset`/`\x`, variables), `.psqlrc`, or fixing connection/auth issues. Includes `(pgNN+)` annotations. The psql client only — SQL syntax -&gt; postgres-sql, server admin -&gt; postgres-admin. ([dashed/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/dashed/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/psql))
